class Solution { public int searchBST ( int root , int val ) { while ( root != null ) { if ( root . val == val ) return root ; else if ( root . val > val ) root = root . left ; else root = root . right ; } return root ; } }

class Solution { public int findLastMinimumValue ( int root ) { if ( root == 0 ) return - 1 ; int ans = Integer . MAX_VALUE ; int min_val = root . val ; Stack < Integer > stack = new Stack < > ( ) ; while ( stack . isEmpty ( ) ) { int curr = stack . peek ( ) ; if ( curr < ans ) continue ; if ( min_val < ans ) { stack . add ( curr . left ) ; stack . add ( curr . right ) ; stack . add ( curr . right ) ; stack . add ( curr . right ) ; stack . add ( curr . right ) ; } } return ans ; } }
class Solution { public int removeNthFromEnd ( Node head , int n ) { if ( head == null ) return null ; int fast = fast . next ; for ( int i = 0 ; i < n ; i ++ ) fast = fast . next ; if ( fast == null ) { head = fast . next ; return head ; } while ( fast . next != null ) { int fast = fast . next ; slow = slow . next ; return head ; } } }
